27:T439,<div class="lab-head"><h4>The closed interval method, live</h4><span class="tag">Lab · move the endpoints</span></div>
<canvas class="tall" id="ex-cv" aria-label="Graph of f on an interval with critical points and endpoints marked, and the absolute max and min highlighted"></canvas>
<div class="controls">
  <label for="ex-fn">f(x)
    <select id="ex-fn">
      <option value="cubic">x³ − 3x² + 1</option>
      <option value="quartic">3x⁴ − 4x³ − 12x² + 1</option>
      <option value="frac">x + 1/x</option>
    </select>
  </label>
  <label for="ex-a">a <input id="ex-a" type="range" min="-2" max="4" step="0.05" value="-0.5"></label>
  <label for="ex-b">b <input id="ex-b" type="range" min="-2" max="4" step="0.05" value="4"></label>
</div>
<div class="readout" id="ex-table"></div>
<p class="note" id="ex-note">Candidates are the critical numbers inside [a, b] (orange) and the two endpoints (gray). The biggest value is the absolute max (green), the smallest the absolute min (red). Shrink the interval and watch the winner change, sometimes to an endpoint.</p>1c:["$","$L15","4",{"children":["$","$L26",null,{"id":"lab-extrema","html":"$27"}]}]
